Sumire protests and tries to quiet her; Player asks what they mean. Murasaki agrees that one person alone being satisfied is intolerable. Haru asks to clean, Honoka mentions ramen/tonkotsu, Rona Nico, and Momoka her entertainment/Chubacs. Yumeno calls the girls’ conflict beautiful. The accusation is Momoka’s interpretation, not an explicit earlier ban on meeting Player or a mutual-romance confession. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0233.json__cbe459d4f340df2a?doc=primary&log=102#/Pages/102` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0233.json__cbe459d4f340df2a?doc=primary&log=116#/Pages/116` (complete P102–116 context). |
| `EV-B0254-033` | 513023004:P117–127 | P117–127: Coney asks Player to do one favor, without the favor’s content being printed in this chapter. After command-only transitions, he is outside worrying whether the camp can end safely. No specific errand, purchase, cancellation, health intervention or completed remedy is yet shown. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0233.json__cbe459d4f340df2a?doc=primary&log=117#/Pages/117` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0233.json__cbe459d4f340df2a?doc=primary&log=127#/Pages/127` (complete P117–127 context). |
| `EV-B0254-034` | 513023005:P0–16 | P0–16: Yumeno announces the morning of camp day7 and says only three days remain, regretting the coming end. Murasaki disagrees and Sumire says they can only endure. Asked about Coney, Haru reports that she is sleeping in her room after saying a lack of doughnuts stopped her head working. Honoka/Haru/Rona name ramen, cleaning and Nico respectively. Yumeno urges persistence, explicitly saying she herself would be sad if the camp stopped. Murasaki would accept cancellation; Momoka reacts to that word. Coney has no own current turn or witnessed sleeping sequence. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0234.json__d20c2ee101f29c44?doc=primary&log=0#/Pages/0` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0234.json__d20c2ee101f29c44?doc=primary&log=16#/Pages/16` (complete P0–16 context). |
| `EV-B0254-035` | 513023005:P17–41 | P17–41: Momoka forms a cancellation plan. She gives Honoka a sensory description of tonkotsu ramen and says quitting would make it available; Honoka reacts strongly. Sumire tells Momoka to stop. Momoka says Sumire could meet Player, which Sumire denies is her assigned abstinence, then names the benefits Haru/Rona could regain and declares everyone would gain. Yumeno argues for continuing for dieting; Momoka points out that she has not slimmed and Yumeno acknowledges the painful point. Momoka chants for cancellation, Yumeno objects, Honoka reports dizziness/hunger and Murasaki becomes resigned. No actual meal, completed cancellation or objective weight/health measurement is shown. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0234.json__d20c2ee101f29c44?doc=primary&log=17#/Pages/17` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0234.json__d20c2ee101f29c44?doc=primary&log=41#/Pages/41` (complete P17–41 context). |
| `EV-B0254-036` | 513023005:P42–62 | P42–62: Hitoha and Futaba appear. Honoka, Haru and Sumire initially call the appearance a hallucination; Honoka notices they all see it. Futaba says it is not one, Hitoha says they are real, and Futaba identifies the actual KARAKURI. Hitoha says they heard about the interesting camp and came to see; Futaba adds a PV shoot on a neighboring island. The presented visitors then express disappointment: Futaba says they had a kind of respect for Nanasta’s never-giving-up strength, Hitoha names that reason, and they suggest the view was mistaken. Their ongoing own turns support a real presented visit rather than a certified shared hallucination; no PV content or exact island map is viewed. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0234.json__d20c2ee101f29c44?doc=primary&log=42#/Pages/42` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0234.json__d20c2ee101f29c44?doc=primary&log=62#/Pages/62` (complete P42–62 context). |
| `EV-B0254-037` | 513023005:P63–78 | P63–78: Haru denies they have failed; Yumeno says they are still just within the limit. Hitoha questions their endurance. Futaba claims that if she understands abstinence is needed for singing, she can endure indefinitely, even a year without food or desire. Murasaki calls that exaggerated and Rona fears collapse. Hitoha explains that she can transfer energy she gains from eating to Futaba, who therefore says her functions would not suffer. They name this シンクロエナジー. Momoka calls it cheating, Sumire likens it to a hermit, and Honoka asks whether it counts as abstinence. Hitoha coughs and shifts to the scale of their resolve. This is an asserted ability plus objections, not a demonstrated one-year fast or independent mechanism proof. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0234.json__d20c2ee101f29c44?doc=primary&log=63#/Pages/63` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0234.json__d20c2ee101f29c44?doc=primary&log=78#/Pages/78` (complete P63–78 context). |
| `EV-B0254-038` | 513023005:P79–97 | P79–97: Futaba says she expected conviction and resolve; Hitoha reconnects the camp to showing their best at the shoot. The group falls silent. The twins again frame failure as unlike Nanasta. Murasaki calls them to wait in her plainer voice and insists they have not failed, appending the formal ending. Honoka distinguishes a wavering heart from failure; Sumire proposes enduring the remaining three days. Hitoha conditionally agrees and Futaba challenges whether they can; Sumire says they can. After the twins’ farewell and Sumire’s note that they have gone, Rona renews effort. Momoka still wants to stop; Haru urges her on, and she reacts reluctantly. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0234.json__d20c2ee101f29c44?doc=primary&log=79#/Pages/79` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0234.json__d20c2ee101f29c44?doc=primary&log=97#/Pages/97` (complete P79–97 context). |
| `EV-B0254-039` | 513023005:P98–111 | P98–111: In the exterior exchange after the scene transition, Hitoha tells Player they gave the requested push; Futaba says they may have overdone it. Player thanks both and credits Coney’s idea while appreciating their quick cooperation. Futaba says she too wants to support their rivals; Hitoha says she does not want the Sisters to lose to anyone but them. Player thanks them again, they answer together, and he says the rest is up to everyone. This privately reveals arranged encouragement without showing the girls hear the explanation or proving every harsh appraisal was insincere. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0234.json__d20c2ee101f29c44?doc=primary&log=98#/Pages/98` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0234.json__d20c2ee101f29c44?doc=primary&log=111#/Pages/111` (complete P98–111 context). |
| `EV-B0254-040` | 513023005:P112–119 | P112–119: After another transition, Honoka rallies everyone for the remaining three days. A strong group cheer is followed by Momoka’s separate weak response. The chapter ends before those days or the shoot are completed; no uniform enthusiasm, final success or full cancellation is supplied. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0234.json__d20c2ee101f29c44?doc=primary&log=112#/Pages/112` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0234.json__d20c2ee101f29c44?doc=primary&log=119#/Pages/119` (complete P112–119 context). |
| `EV-B0254-041` | 513023006:P0–9 | P0–9: Honoka narrates ten long days of endurance, the camp’s end, a brief rest and an apparently safely completed wedding-venue CM shoot, then the end of abstinence and time to return. The later coda explicitly qualifies this general completion account: Yumeno’s participation was deferred because her dress did not fit. No all-participant shoot completion or independent production/performance audit may be inferred from the opening alone. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0235.json__77078150faa4af88?doc=primary&log=0#/Pages/0` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0235.json__77078150faa4af88?doc=primary&log=9#/Pages/9` (complete P0–9 context); later-coda qualification: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0235.json__77078150faa4af88?doc=primary&log=78#/Pages/78` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0235.json__77078150faa4af88?doc=primary&log=98#/Pages/98` (complete P78–98 context). |
| `EV-B0254-042` | 513023006:P10–20 | P10–20: Sumire, Haru and Honoka mark the end with relief. Rona says abstinence is over. Momoka asks whether anime/games/Chubacs are free again, receives Honoka’s yes, celebrates freedom, lists future new/classic media and Chubacs-history plans, and announces goodbye. Native fadeout/SFX stages her exit; no completed home journey, media viewing or new purchase is shown. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0235.json__77078150faa4af88?doc=primary&log=10#/Pages/10` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0235.json__77078150faa4af88?doc=primary&log=20#/Pages/20` (complete P10–20 context). |
| `EV-B0254-043` | 513023006:P21–31 | P21–31: Haru wants to clean the building because it has long bothered her and because using it creates a duty to clean. Sumire offers help; Haru asks to do it alone. Sumire notes her enthusiasm and Rona recalls the long desire. Rona plans to hurry home and watch Seven Sisters live footage; Honoka predicts it may feel special and Rona agrees. No full cleaning, actual video viewing or fresh Seven Sisters performance is presented. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0235.json__77078150faa4af88?doc=primary&log=21#/Pages/21` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0235.json__77078150faa4af88?doc=primary&log=31#/Pages/31` (complete P21–31 context). |
| `EV-B0254-044` | 513023006:P32–44 | P32–44: Murasaki asks whether she can talk normally again, still using the imposed register, and cannot readily recall her prior style. Sumire confirms it was not this. Murasaki tries forceful だぜ endings, dislikes feminine speech practice, then questions whether the attempt is right. Sumire predicts she may eventually return to normal; Murasaki hopes so and announces going home, followed by Honoka’s farewell and native fadeout/SFX. Restoration is not completed merely because the rule ended. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0235.json__77078150faa4af88?doc=primary&log=32#/Pages/32` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0235.json__77078150faa4af88?doc=primary&log=44#/Pages/44` (complete P32–44 context). |
| `EV-B0254-045` | 513023006:P45–58 | P45–58: Sumire proposes stopping at the office before home. Honoka teases that she will see Player; Sumire denies that interpretation. Honoka acknowledges the camp’s painful parts but feels glad she did it, alongside release. Sumire agrees and predicts today’s ramen will be delicious. Honoka hesitates: she reports having lost weight and fears overeating/rebound after such effort; Sumire acknowledges the fear. No quantity, actual later rebound or medical determination is supplied. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0235.json__77078150faa4af88?doc=primary&log=45#/Pages/45` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0235.json__77078150faa4af88?doc=primary&log=58#/Pages/58` (complete P45–58 context). |
| `EV-B0254-046` | 513023006:P59–77 | P59–77: Sumire suggests a reward just for today because she is hungry. Honoka agrees, invites her to a ramen place she knows, and Sumire adds a cake place she knows. Both agree and look forward to it. Sumire tentatively attributes this appreciation to the camp; Honoka agrees. They announce setting off, and native transitions clear the scene. This is a shared plan and represented departure, not a shown meal, calorie total, binge or completed rebound. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0235.json__77078150faa4af88?doc=primary&log=59#/Pages/59` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0235.json__77078150faa4af88?doc=primary&log=77#/Pages/77` (complete P59–77 context). |
| `EV-B0254-047` | 513023006:P78–98 | P78–98: Player’s happy-ending comment is interrupted by Coney. In the office scene she says one person’s shoot was moved to later and initially insists she told him; he says this is the first he has heard, and she concedes she may not have said it. Yumeno appears in a much larger-body drawing; Player does not recognize her, Coney identifies her, and Yumeno explains the wedding dress did not fit. Coney says she inexplicably gained greatly despite tightly managed meals; Yumeno reports eating almost nothing and tentatively explains that living with girls seems to give her highest-grade energy. This is a comic actor-level cause, not an independently established physiological law or proof of secret meals. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0235.json__77078150faa4af88?doc=primary&log=78#/Pages/78` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0235.json__77078150faa4af88?doc=primary&log=98#/Pages/98` (complete P78–98 context). |
| `EV-B0254-048` | 513023006:P99–114 | P99–114: Coney reports the repeat shoot is one week later and missing it would apparently miss the deadline. Player asks whether Yumeno must slim within that week; Coney proposes another camp and he agrees. Yumeno asks about other girls; Player says there will be none, she objects to being alone, and Coney declares Yumeno’s true solitary abstinence camp beginning. Player predicts she may slim quickly in that state and encourages her. The new week, weight outcome, actual solitary regimen and repeat shoot remain uncompleted at the source ending. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0235.json__77078150faa4af88?doc=primary&log=99#/Pages/99` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0235.json__77078150faa4af88?doc=primary&log=114#/Pages/114` (complete P99–114 context). |

Kyoko wonders whether Rena also likes Player; Honoka wonders whether Rena is making an advance toward her and wants her included in the supposed relationship. Renas private thought states her own continuing purpose: protect Players head secret. Player ends with confusion. No actor states or resolves the actual propositions before the chapter closes. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0238.json__0c03d6a509fdf93e?doc=primary&log=82#/Pages/82` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0238.json__0c03d6a509fdf93e?doc=primary&log=93#/Pages/93` (complete P82–93 context). |
| `EV-B0255-025` | 514023604:P0–18 | P0–14: in a hotel-room-type morning setting, Kyoko privately says she slept poorly, wonders how serious Honoka and Rena are and imagines how far Honoka/Player may have progressed, including a possible kiss. These are distressed questions, not an observed kiss or relationship history. Honoka also says in thought she slept poorly and wonders whether she should enter the supposed relationship with Rena/Kyoko. Rena instead comments on the strong wind and caution; Honoka wonders whether that means coming turmoil. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0239.json__aea3005975f85de6?doc=primary&log=0#/Pages/0` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0239.json__aea3005975f85de6?doc=primary&log=18#/Pages/18` (complete P0–18 context). |
| `EV-B0255-026` | 514023604:P19–26 | P19–26: Player says the previous trouble has pushed filming back and asks the trio to work at a faster pace. Rena responds. Honoka asks Player for private consultation, internally says she cannot stay silent, and he agrees to go aside with her. This is an actual request and accepted opportunity; its substantive explanation has not occurred. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0239.json__aea3005975f85de6?doc=primary&log=19#/Pages/19` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0239.json__aea3005975f85de6?doc=primary&log=26#/Pages/26` (complete P19–26 context). |
| `EV-B0255-027` | 514023604:P27–36 | P27–36: Rena privately suspects Honoka has noticed her protection of Players wig and is going to expose the truth. She says she must stop them and calls Players guard weak. Her inner cutaways repeatedly specify of the wig, while Kyoko reads her concern through possible feelings for Player and asks an unfinished question. Renas spoken yes is privately completed as worry about the wig, not a romantic confession. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0239.json__aea3005975f85de6?doc=primary&log=27#/Pages/27` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0239.json__aea3005975f85de6?doc=primary&log=36#/Pages/36` (complete P27–36 context). |
| `EV-B0255-028` | 514023604:P37–56 | P37–52: Rena tells Honoka not to go and to remain with them. Honoka privately reads this as an invitation into the imagined relationship; Kyoko privately reads it as obstructing Honoka/Player. When Player asks what is wrong, Honoka says it is nothing and withdraws the consultation request. Her private uncertainty and Kyokos belief that the other two are competing over Player, plus worry for LeSca, culminate in a grouped cry. Rena asks whether both are all right. No actor has explained the actual propositions to Player or the peers. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0239.json__aea3005975f85de6?doc=primary&log=37#/Pages/37` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0239.json__aea3005975f85de6?doc=primary&log=56#/Pages/56` (complete P37–56 context). |
| `EV-B0255-029` | 514023604:P57–65 | P57–65: the literal 監督 role requests a jump be done again, and the trio answers. A separate 撮影スタッフ role asks Player whether something happened because the performers do not seem able to concentrate. Player agrees/apologizes and privately wonders what is wrong. The director again asks for another take. These are source-stated work appraisals and requests, not analyst review of finished footage or continuous movement. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0239.json__aea3005975f85de6?doc=primary&log=57#/Pages/57` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0239.json__aea3005975f85de6?doc=primary&log=65#/Pages/65` (complete P57–65 context). |
| `EV-B0255-030` | 514023604:P66–68 | P66–68: Players closing parenthetical narration says the shoot continues unsuccessfully and that filming the scene requiring coordination among all three is carried over to the final day. This establishes that specific deferred work, not that every individual shot failed, that the entire PV is cancelled, or that the final day succeeds. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0239.json__aea3005975f85de6?doc=primary&log=66#/Pages/66` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0239.json__aea3005975f85de6?doc=primary&log=68#/Pages/68` (complete P66–68 context). |
| `EV-B0255-031` | 514023605:P0–16 | P0–14: in the room, Honoka asks what happens if they cannot coordinate tomorrow. Kyoko reports hearing there is no reshoot date and speculates the scenes showing them together will be omitted. Honoka mourns that possibility despite this being LeSca. Rena says she will go outside for air and the others acknowledge her departure. Kyoko then considers going out too; her actual presence later at the well, rather than this proposal alone, establishes that she subsequently leaves. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0240.json__cdc29ed239b0cc14?doc=primary&log=0#/Pages/0` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0240.json__cdc29ed239b0cc14?doc=primary&log=16#/Pages/16` (complete P0–16 context). |
| `EV-B0255-032` | 514023605:P17–29 | P17–28: outside on the beach, Rena wonders why relations are strained, considers secrecy as a cause, says she has suffered since that day and privately wishes she had never learned the supposed wig secret. She seeks a change of mood, remembers a received brochure, reads it and reacts. These are her present accounts and judgments; they do not make the hairpiece belief true. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0240.json__cdc29ed239b0cc14?doc=primary&log=17#/Pages/17` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0240.json__cdc29ed239b0cc14?doc=primary&log=29#/Pages/29` (complete P17–29 context). |
| `EV-B0255-033` | 514023605:P30–40 | P30–40: Rena arrives at the old well said to lie in the coastal woods and describes it as a hidden popular spot for shouting secrets. She assumes nobody is listening and prepares to shout Player is—. Honokas arrival interrupts; Renas partly uttered baldness sound is cut into coughing. The assumption of solitude immediately fails. No magical privacy function, well spirit or permanent cure is shown. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0240.json__cdc29ed239b0cc14?doc=primary&log=30#/Pages/30` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0240.json__cdc29ed239b0cc14?doc=primary&log=40#/Pages/40` (complete P30–40 context). |
| `EV-B0255-034` | 514023605:P41–51 | P41–51: Kyoko also arrives. Each member says she came because the brochure described the secret-shouting well. Surprise gives way to a grouped laugh and recognition that everyone has a burden. The source supports their convergence through a shared tourist reference; nobody is shown deliberately following another, arranging an ambush or being summoned by Player. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0240.json__cdc29ed239b0cc14?doc=primary&log=41#/Pages/41` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0240.json__cdc29ed239b0cc14?doc=primary&log=51#/Pages/51` (complete P41–51 context). |
| `EV-B0255-035` | 514023605:P52–63 | P52–63: Kyoko explains she came to shout and make a decision after considering what matters and what to do about a secret that could shake their relationships. She says her answer is decided. Honoka says she has worried too, cannot immediately give an answer but believes she knows what matters. Rena joins the reflection and refers to an important persons large secret. Their degrees of certainty and private referents remain differentiated. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0240.json__cdc29ed239b0cc14?doc=primary&log=52#/Pages/52` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0240.json__cdc29ed239b0cc14?doc=primary&log=63#/Pages/63` (complete P52–63 context). |
| `EV-B0255-036` | 514023605:P64–71 | P64–71: each member starts with her own first-person form, then the literal group label レナ・キョーコ・ホノカ declares LeScaを、大切にしたい. Rena notices that their wishes overlapped; Honoka agrees, Kyoko says this shared value gives her resolve and Rena agrees too. This is a genuine common object before agreement about the facts behind any secret. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0240.json__cdc29ed239b0cc14?doc=primary&log=64#/Pages/64` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0240.json__cdc29ed239b0cc14?doc=primary&log=71#/Pages/71` (complete P64–71 context). |
| `EV-B0255-037` | 514023605:P72–79 | P72–79: Rena says she wants to tell the others what she has hidden despite possible trouble for Player. Kyoko says she will tell what she learned and her true feelings; Honoka also commits to speak. Rena proposes a simultaneous shout toward the well and supplies the count-in, while Kyoko/Honoka assent. Kyokos declared intention to reveal true feelings is not itself a completed confession of her own romantic attachment. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0240.json__cdc29ed239b0cc14?doc=primary&log=72#/Pages/72` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0240.json__cdc29ed239b0cc14?doc=primary&log=79#/Pages/79` (complete P72–79 context). |
| `EV-B0255-038` | 514023605:P80–89 | P80–89: the three printed statements finally specify incompatible propositions: Kyoko says Honoka and Player are mutually in love and in an illicit relationship; Honoka says Rena and Kyoko have such a relationship; Rena says Player wears a wig. Grouped silence/question marks precede direct challenges: Kyoko asks what her supposed relationship with Rena means, Honoka is astonished at her supposed relationship with Player, Rena asks what either of those claims means, and Kyoko/Honoka exclaim at the wig claim. These reactions establish uptake of concrete contents, but the prop, catch and book explanations are not yet reconstructed in this complete chapter. Player is absent from this exchange and receives no shown disclosure. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0240.json__cdc29ed239b0cc14?doc=primary&log=80#/Pages/80` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0240.json__cdc29ed239b0cc14?doc=primary&log=89#/Pages/89` (complete P80–89 context). |
| `EV-B0255-039` | 514023606:P0–10 | P0–10: at the beach, the director approves a take and moves on; the trio responds. Shooting staff praise their coordination and assume Player solved the problem overnight. Player explicitly denies doing that and wonders what happened. Staff forecast that the faster pace will meet the schedule and praise the continuous smiles; Player agrees about the smiles. These are role appraisals and a forecast, not an analyst grade of footage. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0241.json__1f62e98f52cedcfb?doc=primary&log=0#/Pages/0` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0241.json__1f62e98f52cedcfb?doc=primary&log=10#/Pages/10` (complete P0–10 context). |
| `EV-B0255-040` | 514023606:P11–27 | P11–25: the trio keep laughing about the previous days relationship claims and tease each other over Kyoko/Rena and Honoka/Player. Rena says they laughed yesterday, Kyoko says she could not sleep from laughing, and Honoka says Rena would laugh when they tried to sleep. Rena states the learned common wish: however things become, they want to remain LeSca; Kyoko/Honoka emphatically assent. The director calls the last stretch and they answer. The explicit overnight/yesterday link establishes local continuity with the well encounter; it does not expose an unprinted detailed explanation of the prop or book. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0241.json__1f62e98f52cedcfb?doc=primary&log=11#/Pages/11` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0241.json__1f62e98f52cedcfb?doc=primary&log=27#/Pages/27` (complete P11–27 context). |
| `EV-B0255-041` | 514023606:P28–39 | P29–39: back at Nanasta, Coney welcomes them, attributes the excellent final-day report to Player, and Rena qualifies the earlier phase as chaotic. Player asks why their coordination suddenly improved. Honoka hesitates, Kyoko laughs and all three answer secret. Coney says filming ended early and asks about their swimming, wishing she had gone. Early completion/swimming are framed as her return report/question; the actual footage and swimming are not displayed. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0241.json__1f62e98f52cedcfb?doc=primary&log=28#/Pages/28` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0241.json__1f62e98f52cedcfb?doc=primary&log=39#/Pages/39` (complete P28–39 context). |
| `EV-B0255-042` | 514023606:P40–47 | P40–47: Player reports that he was somehow prevented from entering the sea. Rena answers that it was dangerous, and he asks what danger she means; Coney answers noncommittally. Rena announces they will go home, Kyoko and Honoka say farewell and Coney acknowledges them. The ongoing danger language continues the protective hairpiece frame rather than demonstrating an actual aquatic incident. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0241.json__1f62e98f52cedcfb?doc=primary&log=40#/Pages/40` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0241.json__1f62e98f52cedcfb?doc=primary&log=47#/Pages/47` (complete P40–47 context). |
| `EV-B0255-043` | 514023606:P48–62 | P48–62: before departing, Rena tells Player that the trio have learned his secret, says she does not mind and predicts nobody at Nanasta would think badly of it. Honoka says he need not hide it from them. Kyoko hesitantly says it would look cool, and Honoka calls it dandy. Player repeatedly displays confusion and asks what they mean. These are distinct offered assurances and Renas forecast about others, not a verified institution-wide consensus. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0241.json__1f62e98f52cedcfb?doc=primary&log=48#/Pages/48` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0241.json__1f62e98f52cedcfb?doc=primary&log=62#/Pages/62` (complete P48–62 context). |
| `EV-B0255-044` | 514023606:P63–68 | P63–68: Rena, Honoka and Kyoko finally use the explicit baldness predicate in their farewells/assurances. Player reacts in surprise and repeats bald, while Coney also reacts. This is the point at which the claim becomes specifically available to Player in the scene; he learns what they are saying, not that he is actually bald. No one explains Renas original prop misreading to him. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0241.json__1f62e98f52cedcfb?doc=primary&log=63#/Pages/63` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0241.json__1f62e98f52cedcfb?doc=primary&log=68#/Pages/68` (complete P63–68 context). |
| `EV-B0255-045` | 514023606:P69–74 | P69–74: Coney calls the baldness story a scoop and announces she must spread it across Nanasta. Player asks her to wait, then ends in a long written cry asking what on earth they are talking about. The source shows gossip framing and intention, not a completed Nanasta-wide broadcast, a verified new physical condition or a resolved misunderstanding. Coney knew the original prop setup in chapter1; the ending does not state whether she is knowingly extending a gag or has sincerely reinterpreted the new assurances. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0241.json__1f62e98f52cedcfb?doc=primary&log=69#/Pages/69` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0241.json__1f62e98f52cedcfb?doc=primary&log=74#/Pages/74` (complete P69–74 context). |

The speaker returns to Haru with ordinary003_11 assets; Haru asks what happened and why she is lying down. The third round is a draw, leaving KARAKURI’s previously established one-win lead intact; the final two rounds are still unopened. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0245.json__f2ff30f2ade8cb26?doc=primary&log=72#/Pages/72` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0245.json__f2ff30f2ade8cb26?doc=primary&log=78#/Pages/78` (complete P72–78 context). |
| `EV-B0256-025` | 515024205:P0–19 | Sawara announces round4 as a fast-buzzer Nanasta quiz with teams of three. Futaba again assigns Mitsuba as their third, but Haru greets them under her ordinary name/resource. Ume objects both to a Nanasta member taking part in a Nanasta quiz and to Mitsuba already having reverted to Kasukabe. Sawara allows the arrangement as a small detail; Ume rejects that description. Emoco notes KARAKURI’s one-win lead with two events left and says losing here would end their chance. Hina thinks food questions might be possible; Ume becomes pessimistic. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0246.json__de224ffc928a9074?doc=primary&log=0#/Pages/0` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0246.json__de224ffc928a9074?doc=primary&log=19#/Pages/19` (complete P0–19 context). |
| `EV-B0256-026` | 515024205:P20–37 | The quiz begins. Shinju asks QOP drummer Mimori Matsuri’s favorite cocktail. Emoco/Hina find it difficult or unknown. 4U buzzes; Ume answers rum-and-Coke and Shinju marks it correct. Haru learns the fact from the answer. Asked how she knew, Ume says a music-magazine interview mentioned it and explains remembering it because QOP are fellow band musicians despite different musical styles. Hina tentatively accepts and Emoco lets the explanation stand with qualification. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0246.json__de224ffc928a9074?doc=primary&log=20#/Pages/20` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0246.json__de224ffc928a9074?doc=primary&log=37#/Pages/37` (complete P20–37 context). |
| `EV-B0256-027` | 515024205:P38–56 | Question2 asks QOP guitarist Sakaiya Yumeno’s doujin pen name. Hina, Futaba and Haru state ignorance or difficulty. Ume answers Yumeno Michi, written夢乃道, and Shinju marks it correct. Haru praises Ume. Emoco and Hina question her knowledge; Ume denies any deep meaning and says she once looked into another guitarist. Emoco and Hina fall silent and shift outward in native slots; Ume asks why they are moving away. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0246.json__de224ffc928a9074?doc=primary&log=38#/Pages/38` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0246.json__de224ffc928a9074?doc=primary&log=56#/Pages/56` (complete P38–56 context). |
| `EV-B0256-028` | 515024205:P57–73 | Question3 asks which Nanasta idol’s name is carved beneath a slide in Nana Park. Hitoha has no idea and Haru did not know there was such a carving. Ume answers Harumi Sawara; Shinju marks it correct. Haru praises her again. Emoco expresses dismay and Hina asks how much she likes them. Ume calls for the two not to go far away. No source shows who carved the name or how Ume learned this answer. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0246.json__de224ffc928a9074?doc=primary&log=57#/Pages/57` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0246.json__de224ffc928a9074?doc=primary&log=73#/Pages/73` (complete P57–73 context). |
| `EV-B0256-029` | 515024205:P74–88 | After an ellipsis, Hime says the tenth question is correct and declares 4U the decisive winner with all ten answers correct. Only the first three questions/answers are shown in detail. Haru congratulates Ume; Futaba and Hitoha separately acknowledge complete defeat. Ume boasts, then asks why the victory does not bring Emoco/Hina back; they remain silent in the distancing assets and Ume calls after them. Round4 gives each group one win with two earlier draws, leaving the fifth event unresolved. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0246.json__de224ffc928a9074?doc=primary&log=74#/Pages/74` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0246.json__de224ffc928a9074?doc=primary&log=88#/Pages/88` (complete P74–88 context). |
| `EV-B0256-030` | 515024206:P0–27 | Sawara states the entering score as one win, one loss and two draws for each group. She has representatives chosen before explaining the final event. Hina and Emoco entrust4U to Ume, who accepts; Hitoha volunteers for KARAKURI saying she has spent less energy. Sawara then sets a30-minute beach treasure hunt for something shiny to cherish forever, with the shinier treasure winning. Hitoha runs off and Emoco urges the initially confused Ume to start. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0247.json__714f2a8b19bbee02?doc=primary&log=0#/Pages/0` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0247.json__714f2a8b19bbee02?doc=primary&log=27#/Pages/27` (complete P0–27 context). |
| `EV-B0256-031` | 515024206:P28–62 | Ume searches and considers shells. Hitoha tells her she is looking for shiny things and that the sea contains them, even gems, then departs. A splash cue and Ume's statement report Hitoha diving. Ume follows the sea idea; after another transition/splash, she coughs and says she cannot hold her breath, cannot see, finds the water salty and nearly drowned. She fears losing with time left and no satisfactory idea. No underwater view, rescue or diagnosed injury is supplied. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0247.json__714f2a8b19bbee02?doc=primary&log=28#/Pages/28` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0247.json__714f2a8b19bbee02?doc=primary&log=62#/Pages/62` (complete P28–62 context). |
| `EV-B0256-032` | 515024206:P63–72 | Two separate anonymous beachgoers discuss a failed flirt and still enjoying the day with friends. Their unvoiced written exchange prompts Ume's explicit thought about friends and her realization that treasure need not be an object. She is embarrassed but thinks it is her remaining option. The cue's competitive occasion and borrowed trigger are part of the account. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0247.json__714f2a8b19bbee02?doc=primary&log=63#/Pages/63` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0247.json__714f2a8b19bbee02?doc=primary&log=72#/Pages/72` (complete P63–72 context). |
| `EV-B0256-033` | 515024206:P73–101 | Hitoha returns as the deadline nears; Hina worries about Ume and Emoco jokes about a dried shiitake. Ume arrives just before time with apparently empty hands, says she has found treasure, calls Haru and asks for her hand. Ume calls Haru her friend; Hime identifies Haru as the treasure. Emoco asks whether the two bandmates are not Ume's treasure. Ume first agrees with that wording, then says they are beyond ordinary friends, like family. She gives her final answer as precious friends and the family watching over them. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0247.json__714f2a8b19bbee02?doc=primary&log=73#/Pages/73` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0247.json__714f2a8b19bbee02?doc=primary&log=101#/Pages/101` (complete P73–101 context). |
| `EV-B0256-034` | 515024206:P102–128 | Hina responds emotionally and Ume protests that the hug is hot; Emoco gives a small response. Sawara says it looks decided, then declares KARAKURI the winner, astonishing4U. Ume objects that this was the flow in which they should win. Sawara reports that Hitoha found a pearl in the sea and says she likes Shii, choosing Hitoha/pearl. Ume calls this family favoritism; Sawara answers that everyone is a person rather than a thing. Hime confirms KARAKURI as the whole contest winner. Emoco calls the affair farcical, Hina relaxes about the loss and Ume remains frustrated. Shinju observes that4U seem happy despite losing; Haru says she had fun. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0247.json__714f2a8b19bbee02?doc=primary&log=102#/Pages/102` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0247.json__714f2a8b19bbee02?doc=primary&log=128#/Pages/128` (complete P102–128 context). |
| `EV-B0256-035` | 515024206:P129–150 | After the background and costumes change to an indoor coda, Futaba asks Ume for a foot massage and Ume answers. Hitoha alerts Emoco to a phone call; Emoco actually answers it in businesslike language, asking for a project brief before an estimate and beginning contact information before the line is cut in the text. Hina announces tea and cookies and the twins thank her. Hitoha calls4U capable, Futaba agrees, and Hitoha asks for a massage next. Ume accepts but protests this is not manager work; the twins invoke her promise to do anything. Ume vows revenge. Emoco predicts their vocalist's failure to learn means further involvement, Hina finds it fun and not boring, and Ume closes by calling for Hitoha/Futaba to wait and see. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0247.json__714f2a8b19bbee02?doc=primary&log=129#/Pages/129` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0247.json__714f2a8b19bbee02?doc=primary&log=150#/Pages/150` (complete P129–150 context). |

| `EV-B0257-001` | 516024801:P0–21 | Rona, Hime, Murasaki and Matsuri assemble for work. Coney performs a quoted Miki line from the older hit delinquent-school drama スケバン放浪記, then explains its remake and reports that all four Nanasta candidates passed document selection for student-role auditions. This is a reported screening result and proposed next audition, not completed casting. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0248.json__51012339a8df827b?doc=primary&log=0#/Pages/0` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0248.json__51012339a8df827b?doc=primary&log=21#/Pages/21` (complete P0–21 context). |

Hime praises the arrangement, Murasaki becomes conditionally hopeful, Rona remains uncertain, and Matsuri projects a cool elder-sister role. Coney supplies makeup, costumes and audition scripts for a local trial. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0248.json__51012339a8df827b?doc=primary&log=22#/Pages/22` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0248.json__51012339a8df827b?doc=primary&log=50#/Pages/50` (complete P22–50 context). |

The sisters announce ワタシ・愛・forU, Yonhon Ribon version, and count in. The crowd cheers, and Player calls this next playing perfect. This is Player's initial inference, subsequently corrected by the backstage reveal; the analyst has not heard the music. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0259.json__62f71f861465982d?doc=primary&log=37#/Pages/37` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0259.json__62f71f861465982d?doc=primary&log=59#/Pages/59` (complete P37–59 context). |
| `EV-B0258-051` | 517025406:P60–80 | The sisters thank the audience, who praise Yonhon Ribon. Sawara announces that Yonhon Ribon is disbanding after this one song, saying they may not be a rock-band type. Kajika calls it enjoyable but invokes musical differences and says they will return to ordinary Sanbon Ribon; Shinju asks for continued support of Sanbon Ribon, and all say goodbye. The audience expresses regret and further praise. The source establishes a local one-song project and its announced closure, not Sanbon Ribon's dissolution or a permanent four/six-member lineup. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0259.json__62f71f861465982d?doc=primary&log=60#/Pages/60` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0259.json__62f71f861465982d?doc=primary&log=80#/Pages/80` (complete P60–80 context). |
| `EV-B0258-052` | 517025406:P81–90 | Backstage, Player praises the playing and is surprised to find 4U. Ume says the players were 4U. Emoco specifies that Yonhon Ribon played only the first intro, and Hina says 4U played from behind the scenes after that. Player recognizes why it sounded so good. Ume nevertheless praises the novices for copying the intro; Emoco explains that the short time led them to take the rest. The later account corrects attribution of the full performance without erasing the sisters' bounded work. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0259.json__62f71f861465982d?doc=primary&log=81#/Pages/81` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0259.json__62f71f861465982d?doc=primary&log=90#/Pages/90` (complete P81–90 context). |
| `EV-B0258-053` | 517025406:P91–100 | Hina says playing hidden was fun. Ume says she prefers being the visible one and treats the effort as a favor owed, which Player acknowledges. The crowd continues calling for Yonhon Ribon, and Player worries how to handle the strong response. No public explanation of the hidden players or later managerial resolution is shown. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0259.json__62f71f861465982d?doc=primary&log=91#/Pages/91` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0259.json__62f71f861465982d?doc=primary&log=100#/Pages/100` (complete P91–100 context). |
| `EV-B0258-054` | 517025406:P101–112 | Several days later, 4U is in a dressing room. Ume complains that time spent on the sisters made the last days busy. Emoco says the venture nearly interfered with their own live; Hina says both practice and hidden playing were fun, and Emoco deliberately does not deny that. Emoco then asks whether Ume finally asked about the earlier matter. Their own professional costs and enjoyment coexist; near interference is not an actually disrupted live. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0259.json__62f71f861465982d?doc=primary&log=101#/Pages/101` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0259.json__62f71f861465982d?doc=primary&log=112#/Pages/112` (complete P101–112 context). |
| `EV-B0258-055` | 517025406:P113–133 | A retrospectively presented return to the earlier one-week-remaining conversation now continues beyond the old HoloCon request. Ume wants to contact the fish-shop pupil to tell her to perfect only the intro, with 4U playing the continuation from behind. Emoco questions her sanity and her mistaken idiom, then asks why Ume does not contact Kajika herself. Ume admits she does not know Kajika's contact details. This plan is first disclosed to the reader here; it does not rewrite cases 4–5 as if they already supplied it. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0259.json__62f71f861465982d?doc=primary&log=113#/Pages/113` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0259.json__62f71f861465982d?doc=primary&log=133#/Pages/133` (complete P113–133 context). |
| `EV-B0258-056` | 517025406:P134–137 | Back in the dressing room, Ume says she still has not asked for the contact, may not need to communicate again, and can ask Kasukabe if necessary. Emoco sighs. Kasukabe resolves through existing frozen identity authority to Haru, who is only a proposed future route: the source supplies no actual query, response, address transfer or Haru own turn. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0259.json__62f71f861465982d?doc=primary&log=134#/Pages/134` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0259.json__62f71f861465982d?doc=primary&log=137#/Pages/137` (complete P134–137 context). |
| `EV-B0258-057` | 517025406:P138–155 | An unnamed staff member brings a package addressed to 4U. Hina identifies it as from Nanasta and opens it to find a letter and other contents. The script presents Shinju's letter: thanks for the live, apology for repeated unreasonable demands, and an account of enjoyable, fruitful two weeks for Sanbon Ribon. The letter identifies Shinju's hair clip for Hina, Kajika's ribbon for Ume, and Sawara's stuffed toy for Emoco, offering use if they like them. This is mediated letter content, not Shinju physically entering the room. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0259.json__62f71f861465982d?doc=primary&log=138#/Pages/138` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0259.json__62f71f861465982d?doc=primary&log=155#/Pages/155` (complete P138–155 context). |
| `EV-B0258-058` | 517025406:P156–164 | Ume verbally dismisses the ribbon and calls Kajika an unfit, expelled pupil. The actual inserted still depicts a turquoise fish-pattern ribbon attached in purple hair, and Hina says it suits Ume. Hina intends to wear the fish hair clip for the live; the inserted still depicts it in her hair. Emoco calls the fish stuffed toy suitable as a bass attachment, and its still depicts it at a pink instrument surface. Ume then says they should go show the others a real rock band, and Hina calls her a teacher after all. The source juxtaposes rejection talk with visible adoption and ongoing teacher framing; it does not establish an administrative expulsion or the completion of the ensuing live. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0259.json__62f71f861465982d?doc=primary&log=156#/Pages/156` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0259.json__62f71f861465982d?doc=primary&log=164#/Pages/164` (complete P156–164 context). |
| `EV-B0258-059` | 517025406:P165–174 | Emoco instructs Ume to send Kajika a proper thank-you email. Hina offers to send the contact later. Ume expresses nervousness before the live; Emoco teases the teacher who is nervous toward her pupil, Ume protests, and Hina laughs. Contact transfer, email dispatch, receipt and the forthcoming 4U performance remain unshown. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0259.json__62f71f861465982d?doc=primary&log=165#/Pages/165` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0259.json__62f71f861465982d?doc=primary&log=174#/Pages/174` (complete P165–174 context). |
